**[00:33:00.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000168)  
Excuse me, Yosef Henry.

**[00:33:02.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000169)  
I'd like to acknowledge the presence of my colleague, Senator Rafi Tupo.

**[00:33:06.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000170)  
Thank you, sir, for joining us.

**[00:33:08.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000171)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:33:09.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000172)  
Yes, please proceed.

**[00:33:10.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000173)  
Tapusin na po natin itong presentation.

**[00:33:11.940] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000174)  
Good afternoon, Senator Tupo.

**[00:33:14.900] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000175)  
For NHA, the agency was allocated 3.897 billion for 2026

**[00:33:22.600] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000176)  
and will be allocated.

**[00:33:24.940] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000177)  
For NHA, the agency was allocated 2.274 billion for next year

**[00:33:28.620] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000178)  
compared to its original request of 44.732 billion.

**[00:33:35.200] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000179)  
For the Social Housing Finance Corporation,

**[00:33:38.660] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000180)  
an amount of 166 million was allocated this year

**[00:33:42.060] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000181)  
and the same amount will be allocated next year.

**[00:33:45.580] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000182)  
However, their original request was 4.666 billion.

**[00:33:50.660] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000183)  
For National Home Mortgage Corporation,

**[00:33:54.940] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000184)  
they were not allocated any fund this year.

**[00:33:57.500] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000185)  
For next year, they will be allocated 450,000.

**[00:34:02.680] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000186)  
Tama po yun.

**[00:34:03.340] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000187)  
Thousand lang po yun.

**[00:34:04.820] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000188)  
And the request was actually 4.7 billion.

**[00:34:09.440] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000189)  
In the case of the Home Development Mutual Fund or Pag-ibig,

**[00:34:13.800] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000190)  
wala pong location or hinihinging amount tayo sa GAA

**[00:34:17.460] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000191)  
but basically based on their mandate,

**[00:34:20.140] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000192)  
they're able to address the housing needs

**[00:34:22.120] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000193)  
and they're able to provide

**[00:34:24.500] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000194)  
affordable housing

**[00:34:26.240] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000195)  
by trying to reduce the interest rates

**[00:34:30.580] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000196)  
for social, economic,

**[00:34:33.700] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000197)  
up to 10 million pesos.

**[00:34:38.360] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000198)  
In addition, we are pleased to inform you

**[00:34:41.860] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000199)  
that the active members of Pag-ibig

**[00:34:44.440] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000200)  
has reached 16.79 million.

**[00:34:46.540] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000201)  
They have allocated 4.08 billion

**[00:34:50.620] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000202)  
for 15 projects for 4 PH

**[00:34:53.800] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000203)  
under the DDHC.

**[00:34:54.500] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000204)  
And their dividend yield this year

**[00:34:57.560] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000205)  
is a high 7.12%.

**[00:35:01.120] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000206)  
Let us now go to the housing situation

**[00:35:06.360] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000207)  
and accomplishment.

**[00:35:08.320] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000208)  
We wish to inform the committee

**[00:35:10.600] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000209)  
that as of May this year,

**[00:35:14.320] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000210)  
the total average accomplishment

**[00:35:19.260] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000211)  
was 114,000 per annum

**[00:35:21.500] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000212)  
for a total of 333,000.

**[00:35:24.500] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000213)  
In the period between June 2026 to August 2026,

**[00:35:32.140] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000214)  
a total of 279,588 units

**[00:35:36.420] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000215)  
or an average of 233,670 per annum.

**[00:35:42.100] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000216)  
The breakdown of the modalities

**[00:35:44.920] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000217)  
mentioned by the Secretary

**[00:35:46.120] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000218)  
are found on the right side of the slide.

**[00:35:50.860] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000219)  
As discussed by our Secretary,

**[00:35:53.080] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000220)  
we have actually expanded

**[00:35:54.280] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000221)  
the 4 PH

**[00:35:55.960] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000222)  
to allow more housing options

**[00:35:59.380] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000223)  
for the Filipino families.

**[00:36:01.140] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000224)  
In addition to the sole modality

**[00:36:03.160] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000225)  
of vertical housing,

**[00:36:05.080] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000226)  
the homeownership now allows

**[00:36:06.760] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000227)  
members or beneficiaries

**[00:36:09.180] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000228)  
to cover horizontal housing,

**[00:36:12.480] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000229)  
real and other properties

**[00:36:13.740] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000230)  
acquired or ROPA,

**[00:36:15.560] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000231)  
Pag-ibig low-interest special programs,

**[00:36:18.320] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000232)  
and the high-density housing

**[00:36:19.800] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000233)  
of the Social Housing Finance Corporation.

**[00:36:22.920] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000234)  
We also now include

**[00:36:23.920] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000235)  
rental housing

**[00:36:25.980] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000236)  
for those who prefer not to purchase

**[00:36:28.620] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000237)  
or have the difficulty purchasing,

**[00:36:31.680] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000238)  
as well as provide modular shelter housing

**[00:36:34.400] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000239)  
or temporary housing

**[00:36:36.080] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000240)  
for those disaster-affected areas.

**[00:36:39.720] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000241)  
In addition, since last year,

**[00:36:42.120] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000242)  
the community mortgage program

**[00:36:44.940] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000243)  
or enhanced community mortgage program

**[00:36:47.300] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000244)  
has actually been revived

**[00:36:49.800] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000245)  
under the new leadership

**[00:36:51.920] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000246)  
of this administration.

**[00:36:53.920] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000247)  
In addition,

**[00:36:55.700] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000248)  
presidential proclamations

**[00:36:57.640] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000249)  
and non-socialized housing

**[00:36:59.120] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000250)  
enrolled under 4 PH

**[00:37:01.200] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000251)  
has actually been undertaken also

**[00:37:03.020] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000252)  
by the middle of 2025.

**[00:37:07.500] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000253)  
Let us now glance through

**[00:37:10.400] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000254)  
the 2025 Dissued Performance.

There are several programs

**[00:37:15.240] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000256)  
and we will cover each of the programs.

**[00:37:17.460] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000257)  
For the Human Settlements

**[00:37:18.480] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000258)  
and Urban Development Coordinating Program,

**[00:37:21.400] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000259)  
the strategies we adopted was

**[00:37:23.060] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000260)

to include the National Resettlement Policy Framework,

**[00:37:27.180] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000262)

**[00:37:29.420] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000263)

**[00:37:33.280] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000264)

**[00:37:44.020] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000268)

**[00:37:46.620] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000269)  
in many of the resettlement sites.

**[00:37:49.640] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000270)  
Last year, we were also able to issue

**[00:37:52.180] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000271)  
213 certificates of entitlements

**[00:37:53.920] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000272)  
under presidential proclamations and handed a total of 365 million for the Integrated Shelter Assistance Program. Our national drive against professional squatters and squatting syndicates

also covered

**[00:38:12.980] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000274)  
39 cases processed,

and 200 active

**[00:38:22.740] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000278)  
In the case of the National Resettlement Policy Framework,

**[00:38:23.700] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000279)  
we also provided

**[00:38:23.900] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000280)  
the Homeowners Association

**[00:38:27.440] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000282)  
We are pleased to inform you

**[00:38:50.160] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000291)  
We've also come out with policies

**[00:38:52.100] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000292)

**[00:38:53.220] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000293)  
We've also come out with policies

For the Housing and Real Estate

Development Regulation Program,

We were also able

With the support also

48-2025

We are also pleased

**[00:46:25.120] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000506)

**[00:46:40.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000507)  
Now, number one,

**[00:46:43.820] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000508)

**[00:46:54.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000509)

**[00:47:05.760] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000510)  
Mr. Chairman, the GM of NHA will answer the question.

**[00:47:09.680] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000511)  
Mr. Chair, good afternoon.

**[00:47:11.620] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000512)  
To answer that, Mr. Chair, actually,

**[00:47:13.820] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000513)  
bago po kami mag-start ng project,

**[00:47:16.200] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000514)  
iba-iba pong programa

**[00:47:17.820] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000515)  
ang mga proyekto po ng NHA.

**[00:47:19.660] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000516)  
Meron pong resettlement,

**[00:47:21.660] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000517)  
meron pong calamity. So, bago po kami

**[00:47:23.940] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000518)  
mag-start, meron na po talagang listahan

**[00:47:26.060] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000519)  
ng mga beneficiaris.

**[00:47:27.960] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000520)  
Wherein, itong listahan po na to,

**[00:47:30.200] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000521)  
galing po ng LGU,

**[00:47:32.140] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000522)  
vinavalidate po ng OCD,

**[00:47:34.960] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000523)  
yung mga

**[00:47:35.540] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000524)  
biktima po ng bagyo o trahedya,

**[00:47:38.500] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000525)  
saka pinapasa po sa amin.

**[00:47:39.860] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000526)  
So, vinavalidate lang po namin ito

**[00:47:42.380] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000527)  
based sa

**[00:47:43.820] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000528)  
hindi po sila naakaulit.

**[00:47:46.600] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000529)  
Kumbaga, hindi po po sila

**[00:47:48.700] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000530)  
nabigyan ng bahay before.

**[00:47:50.720] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000531)  
But with regards to the original list,

**[00:47:52.240] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000532)  
galing po yan sa LGU.

**[00:47:53.520] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000533)  
Correct. Thank you, sir. Thank you.

**[00:47:55.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000534)  
Actually, kaibigan ko ito si Mr. Joben Tai.

**[00:47:57.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000535)

**[00:47:58.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000536)

**[00:47:59.900] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000537)

**[00:48:03.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000539)

**[00:48:05.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000540)

**[00:48:12.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000544)

**[00:48:15.820] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000547)

**[00:48:18.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000549)

**[00:48:22.180] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000551)

**[00:48:26.120] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000553)

**[00:48:28.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000555)

**[00:48:29.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000556)  
eh, puro mga

**[00:48:31.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000557)  
yung nababalitaan nyo

**[00:48:33.460] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000558)

**[00:48:35.340] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000559)

**[00:48:36.600] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000560)  
bata-bata ni chairman,

**[00:48:37.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000561)  
et cetera, et cetera.

**[00:48:38.860] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000562)  
So, nanggali mismo sa iyo,

**[00:48:40.840] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000563)

**[00:48:41.880] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000564)

**[00:48:43.820] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000565)

**[01:00:14.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001205)  
Now, as we await other colleagues, the Chair would like to ask some questions.

**[01:00:20.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001206)  
First, regarding expanded 4PH as presented and mentioned by the Secretary

**[01:00:27.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001207)  
in the presentation, is this still the government's flagship housing program?

**[01:00:34.040] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001208)  
Yes, it is still the flagship program for the housing.

**[01:00:39.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001209)  
Yes, according to data, expanded 4PH program targets a delivery of 301,040 units by June 2028.

**[01:00:47.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001210)  
However, as of June 2026, only 41.7% of this target has reportedly been delivered,

**[01:00:54.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001211)  
with only two years remaining before the target date.

**[01:00:56.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001212)  
How common?

**[01:00:57.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001214)  
How confident is DHSUD that it can deliver the remaining 175,000 or so housing units?

**[01:01:04.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001215)  
Or what's the new target? Have we adjusted our target?

**[01:01:08.380] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001216)  
Number one, Mr. Chairman, the target is still the same.

**[01:01:11.640] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001217)  
Now, we are confident that we will hit that, we will be able to comply with that commitment.

**[01:01:17.180] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001218)  
As the numbers will show, yung jump natin from the first three years,

**[01:01:21.720] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001219)  
then when we expanded the 4PH by adding modalities,

**[01:05:52.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001276)

**[01:05:57.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001277)

**[01:32:00.780] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002216)  
Ang ehensyang ito ay mahalagang tungkulin at yakin na ang bawat pamilyang Pilipino ay may disenting tirahan.

**[01:32:07.700] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002217)

**[01:32:13.260] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002218)

**[01:32:14.820] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002219)

**[01:32:20.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002220)  
Kaya bawat pisong inilaan natin para sa pabahay ay dapat direktang mapakinabangan ng mga pamilyang higit na nangangailangan.

**[01:32:28.360] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002221)  
Lalong-lalo na po yung mga mahirap nating kababayan.

**[01:32:31.460] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002222)  
The proposed 2027 NEP allocates a total budget of 5.499 billion for the Dishud.

**[01:32:40.280] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002223)  
This is 9.5 lower than the 2026 total program budget.

**[01:32:44.820] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002224)  
Of 6.079 billion.

**[01:32:49.740] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002225)  
While the proposed budget is reduced, the need for decent and affordable housing has not diminished.

**[01:32:59.020] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002226)  
In fact, with the rising cost of land and construction materials at sa taas po ng presyo ng bilihin,

**[01:33:05.280] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002227)  
lalong nagiging mahirap para sa ordinaryong Pilipino na magkaroon ng sariling tahanan.

**[01:33:10.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002228)

**[01:33:14.820] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002229)  
Sunog, baha, lindol, putok ng bulkan, mga sakuna. Maraming sa ating mga kababayan po ang nangangailangan ng tulong sa pabahay.

**[01:33:23.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002230)  
Sana po ay mas paigtingin pa ng Dishud ang kanilang mga programang magbibigay tulong sa kanila.

**[01:33:28.400] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002231)  
Lalo na yung mga walang matakbuhan kundi po ang gobyerno.

**[01:33:32.380] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002232)  
Meron lang po akong katanungan about the housing backlog.

**[01:33:36.880] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002233)  
Mr. Chair, the magnitude of our country's housing problem remains a serious concern.

**[01:33:41.940] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002234)  
For years, government estimates placed a...

**[01:33:44.820] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002235)  
the country's housing need at around 6.5 million units.

**[01:33:50.100] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002236)  
With the earlier projections warning that housing requirements could reach as high as 22 million units by 2040

**[01:33:59.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002237)  
if left not addressed.

**[01:34:03.980] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002238)  
Kung hindi po ito ma-address, it could reach as high as 22 million units

**[01:34:08.620] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002239)  
ang housing requirements na backlog.

**[01:34:12.200] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002240)  
Dishud has recently stated that the actual...

**[01:34:14.820] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002241)  
the housing backlog is approximately 2.2 million units.

**[01:34:22.620] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002242)  
Sino pong sasagot nito? Dishud, no? Sekretary Aliling.

**[01:34:27.020] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002243)  
Mr. Chair, based on the latest PSA study,

**[01:34:32.500] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002244)  
ang forecast natin sa housing need by 2028 is 3.7 million units.

**[01:34:39.280] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002245)  
Broken down as follows.

**[01:34:41.260] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002246)  
Number one, 1.1 million.

**[01:34:44.820] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002247)  
Ito po yung may mga sariling tahanan, unit kailangan i-rehabilitate or ayusin.

**[01:34:50.100] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002248)  
So most likely, nandyan po sa 1.1 million na yan, yung mga informal settler families.

**[01:34:55.580] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002249)  
Ang solution po na nakikita natin dyan under the 4PH is the community mortgage program.

**[01:35:01.380] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002250)  
Para pag na-award po sa kanila yung kanilang mga tinitirhan,

**[01:35:05.160] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002251)  
mas mainganyo silang magpaayos, magpa-repair ng mga tahanan nila.

**[01:35:09.980] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002252)  
Then the second is the 2.2 million na tinatawag natin double up.

**[01:35:14.820] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002253)  
Ito pong double up, ito po yung may multiple families living under one roof.

**[01:35:20.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002254)  
So ito po, buong span po ito, hindi lang ito socialized housing, pati open market kasama rito.

**[01:35:29.540] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002255)  
So kahit na may kaya yung pamilya, basta more than one family ang nakatira, kasama po sila dito sa 2.2.

**[01:35:38.640] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002256)  
Then the 400,000 remaining of the 3.7 million is yung forecast nung neto,

**[01:35:44.660] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002257)  
na masisira at maayos.

**[01:35:47.500] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002258)  
So yung po yung 3.7 million, ang gusto lang po natin yung highlight dito,

**[01:35:52.120] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002259)  
na yung 4PH po, pag na-expand natin ito, napalago natin, naparami natin yung output nito,

**[01:35:59.020] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002260)  
should ultimately help resolve the projected housing need of 3.7 million by 2028.

**[01:36:07.380] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002261)  
Salamat. Pwede natin pasingitin muna natin ang Mr. Trabajador ng Senado.

**[01:36:12.700] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002262)

**[01:36:14.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002263)

**[01:36:44.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002270)

**[01:36:51.260] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002271)

**[01:36:56.080] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002272)

**[01:37:01.920] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002273)

**[01:37:10.560] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002275)

**[01:37:14.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002276)

**[01:37:22.580] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002277)

**[01:37:32.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002278)

**[01:37:36.140] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002279)

**[01:37:39.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002280)

**[01:37:44.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002281)

**[01:37:48.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002282)

**[01:37:57.820] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002284)

**[01:38:01.320] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002285)

**[01:38:30.580] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002289)

**[01:38:43.340] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-002292)

**[01:38:44.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002293)

**[01:38:49.320] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002294)

**[01:38:55.500] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002295)

**[01:39:01.100] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002296)

**[01:39:08.080] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002297)

**[01:39:10.880] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002298)

**[01:39:14.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002299)

**[01:39:22.300] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002300)  
Mr. Chair, I think DBM representative is...

**[01:39:24.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002301)

**[01:39:26.440] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002302)

**[01:39:28.580] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002303)

**[01:39:31.520] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002304)

**[01:39:33.320] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-002305)  
Mr. Chair, if I may add...

**[01:39:34.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002306)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:39:36.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002307)  
The challenge actually is...

**[01:39:37.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002308)  
Ano nga yung kanina, dilema mo rin?

**[01:39:38.980] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-002309)  
Yeah, because puro SARO po, wala pong cash.

**[01:39:42.780] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-002310)  
And parang...

**[01:39:44.660] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-002311)

**[01:39:48.520] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-002312)

**[01:39:54.640] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-002313)  
So, yung cash ko mas maliit compared sa institutional commitments that I get.

**[01:40:01.640] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-002314)  
So, I cannot fill it up if my NIP is smaller than the gaako.

**[01:40:08.700] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002315)

**[01:40:11.580] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002316)  
Madam Director Lulu?

**[01:40:14.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002317)  
Ano pong status po nito?

**[01:40:16.460] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002318)  
Kasi halos kalahati, 50% na yata yung trabaho sa maramag bukid nun po ito,

**[01:40:23.420] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002319)  
tsaka keson bukid nun.

**[01:40:26.320] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002320)  
Yung mga tao, excited na excited na po.

**[01:40:28.720] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002321)

**[01:40:34.160] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002322)  
Kaya sabi namin, we want to make sure that these are properly implemented

**[01:40:38.260] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002323)  
or else, apektado na naman tayo sa social media yung buong bansa,

**[01:40:43.860] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002324)  
administration.

**[01:40:44.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002325)  
So, Director, what is the status of that?

**[01:40:47.340] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-002326)  
Yes, good afternoon, Mr. Chair, and Your Honor.

**[01:40:51.080] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-002327)  
Although narilis po namin ang SARO, which pwede na po sila mag-obligate,

**[01:40:56.920] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-002328)

**[01:41:03.380] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-002329)

**[01:41:09.900] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-002330)

**[01:41:12.980] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-002331)  
Pwede niya pong kuhanin doon.

**[01:41:14.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002332)  
So, how do we do this?

**[01:41:18.040] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002333)  
He said, she said.

**[01:41:20.080] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002334)  
Kaya ba yan, GM?

**[01:41:21.580] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002335)

**[01:41:22.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002336)

**[01:41:26.560] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002337)  
Madam Lulu.

**[01:41:30.140] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-002338)  
Your Honor, kasi based on the financial statement po nila submitted as of June,

**[01:41:34.260] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-002339)  
meron po silang cash eh, na 8.1 billion, na pwede po nilang gamitin pag-augment doon sa allotment na narilis po ng DBM.

**[01:41:43.200] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-002340)  
Tapos kinigitan din po namin, sir, yung mga ending cash balance nila eh,

**[01:41:47.600] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-002341)

**[01:41:52.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002342)

**[01:41:55.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002343)

**[01:42:00.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002344)

**[01:42:04.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002345)

**[01:42:04.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002346)

**[01:42:07.220] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-002348)

**[01:42:08.900] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-002349)

**[01:42:09.580] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-002350)

**[01:42:13.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002351)

**[01:42:16.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002352)

**[01:42:24.360] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002353)

**[01:42:30.820] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002354)

**[01:42:36.820] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002355)

**[01:42:38.440] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002356)

**[01:42:42.080] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002357)

**[01:42:45.440] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002358)

**[01:42:48.180] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-002359)

**[02:43:03.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003837)

**[02:43:05.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003838)

**[02:43:07.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003839)

**[02:43:09.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003840)

**[02:43:13.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003842)

**[02:43:15.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003843)

**[02:43:17.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003844)

**[02:43:19.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003845)  
under the temporary

**[02:43:20.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003846)  
license to sell Department Circular

**[02:43:23.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003847)  
No. 2026-12.

**[02:43:26.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003848)  
Developers are

**[02:43:27.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003849)  
allowed to conduct pre-selling activities

**[02:43:28.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003850)  
through a temporary license to sell.

**[02:43:30.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003851)  
However, the pre-selling

**[02:43:32.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003852)  
earnings and proceeds shall be deposited

**[02:43:34.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003853)  
in ESCRO and

**[02:43:36.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003854)  
cannot be withdrawn until a regular

**[02:43:38.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003855)  
license to sell is

**[02:43:40.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003856)  
secured. I think we understand

**[02:43:43.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003857)  
the intent. But anyway,

**[02:43:45.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003858)  
ang ano lang natin

**[02:43:46.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003859)  
has that they should assess whether

**[02:43:48.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003860)  
these regulations could inadvertently

**[02:43:50.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003861)  
slow down housing construction,

**[02:43:53.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003862)  
particularly affordable and socialized

**[02:43:54.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003863)  
housing because money collected from

**[02:43:56.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003864)  
pre-selling is locked in ESCRO and it cannot

**[02:43:58.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003865)  
be used to circulate.

**[02:44:01.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003866)  
Could this

**[02:44:03.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003867)  
also slow down economic activity

**[02:44:04.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003868)  
considering that these funds could otherwise

**[02:44:07.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003869)  
be used for construction

**[02:44:09.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003870)  
jobs and other economic activities?

**[02:44:11.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003871)  
Meron na ba tayong assessment

**[02:44:12.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003872)  
whether these regulations could

**[02:44:14.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003873)  
have inadvertently

**[02:44:17.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003874)  
slow down the construction

**[02:44:19.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003875)  
activities?

**[02:44:20.580] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003876)  
Mr. Chairman, regarding the

**[02:44:22.920] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003877)  
license to sell, there are three types of

**[02:44:25.000] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003878)  
license to sell. Number one is the

**[02:44:26.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003879)  
regular license to sell. Ito yung pag

**[02:44:28.480] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003880)  
100% complied ka na.

**[02:44:30.820] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003881)  
Then second is the conditional license

**[02:44:32.680] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003882)  
to sell. Ito, kumpleto

**[02:44:34.800] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003883)  
ka na except yung balance

**[02:44:36.680] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003884)  
housing requirement.

**[02:44:38.720] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003885)  
So both the regular and the conditional

**[02:44:40.740] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003886)  
license to sell,

**[02:44:44.020] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003887)  
magagamit po

**[02:44:45.020] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003888)  
ng developers yung proceeds

**[02:44:46.840] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003889)  
nila sa pinagbentahan.

**[02:44:50.260] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003890)  
Ngayon,

**[02:44:51.460] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003891)  
dahil last

**[02:44:52.720] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003892)  
option natin is always to deny.

**[02:44:54.560] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003893)  
Kaya nga sinasabi natin, mababa yung

**[02:44:56.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003894)  
rate natin. May mga items

**[02:44:58.740] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003895)  
kaming in-identify.

**[02:45:00.000] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003896)  
na kahit kulang ka, you are given one year to comply.

**[02:45:05.000] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003897)  
Ang ini-issue po dito sa ganitong kaso ay yung temporary license to sell.

**[02:45:10.820] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003898)  
Minamanage po natin dito yung risk nung mga bumili.

**[02:45:15.240] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003899)  
So ang downside nito or nakikita ng mga developers na disadvantage

**[02:45:19.200] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003900)  
ng temporary license to sell is hindi nila nagagamit yung pinagbentahan nila

**[02:45:22.980] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003901)  
na ilalagay ito sa escrow account.

**[02:45:25.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003902)  
So ngayon, ito po hindi natin,

**[02:45:29.760] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003903)  
nagsabit po kami ng position paper dito sa Office of the President.

**[02:45:33.460] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003904)  
Sinasabi po namin na hindi natin pwede ilagay sa risk yung mga buyers natin.

**[02:45:39.180] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003905)  
Ang pinapropose kasi ng ibang mga developers is to allow them to release these funds

**[02:45:44.580] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003906)  
progressively with the construction.

**[02:45:47.440] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003907)  
Sinasabi nila, kunyari 25% na yung construction,

**[02:45:50.480] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003908)  
payagan sila mag-draw ng 25%.

**[02:45:53.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003909)  
Progress ano? Progress rin?

**[02:45:55.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003910)  
As far as the home buyer is concerned,

**[02:45:58.340] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003911)  
hindi po ganito ang pananaw nila dyan.

**[02:46:00.340] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003912)  
Pag dumating ang point, for whatever reason,

**[02:46:02.880] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003913)  
hindi na-comply yung dapat nga i-comply in one year.

**[02:46:05.920] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003914)  
Kung magre-refund po tayo sa mga buyer,

**[02:46:09.560] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003915)  
wala na po yung pera ng i-re-refund ng buyer.

**[02:46:13.180] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003916)  
Ito po yung pinaprevent natin dito.

**[02:46:15.140] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003917)  
Malaki sa kompromiso yung mga buyer.

**[02:46:17.060] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003918)  
Ngayon, ito pong one year, kontrolado naman doon ng developer.

**[02:46:21.300] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003919)  
They were given this option kung ayaw nilang madenay yung application.

**[02:46:25.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003920)  
May mga buyer, may mga developer school kasi

**[02:46:28.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003921)  
na tingin nila in one or two months, mako-comply na nila.

**[02:46:32.260] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003922)  
So nag-a-avail sila nilang itong temporal license to sell

**[02:46:34.780] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003923)  
para makakolekta na sila.

**[02:46:36.680] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003924)  
So ito is a compromise.

**[02:46:39.020] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003925)  
We are maintaining our stand on this

**[02:46:42.820] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003926)  
to protect the home buyers, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:46:45.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003927)  
Okay, how many, just an idea,

**[02:46:47.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003928)  
how many ongoing projects are currently operating

**[02:46:48.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003929)  
on temporary licenses?

**[02:46:50.820] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003930)  
Less than 20, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:46:52.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003931)  
Less than 20.

**[02:46:53.680] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003932)  
Tama.

**[02:46:54.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003933)  
Ito.

**[02:46:55.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003934)  
Anyway, you mentioned already

**[02:46:56.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003935)  
the Balanced Housing Development Program.

**[02:46:58.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003936)  
The objective of the Balanced Housing Development Program

**[02:47:00.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003937)  
is not merely to collect compliance payments for developers,

**[02:47:03.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003938)  
but to ensure that these resources

**[02:47:05.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003939)  
translate into actual housing.

**[02:47:07.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003940)  
What is the current status of the escrow accounts established

**[02:47:10.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003941)  
under the Balanced Housing Development Program?

**[02:47:12.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003942)  
How much has been collected from developers to date?

**[02:47:15.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003943)  
And what is the total balance of these accounts?

**[02:47:18.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003944)  
So yung,

**[02:47:18.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003945)  
what is the status of the escrow accounts established

**[02:47:22.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003946)  
under the BHDP?

**[02:47:24.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003947)  
The total escrow fund

**[02:47:26.920] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003948)  
is around 15 billion collected.

**[02:47:30.360] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003949)  
This is based on the 25% IRR,

**[02:47:35.480] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003950)  
which was later

**[02:47:36.880] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003951)  
issued by DOJ as,

**[02:47:40.360] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003952)  
DOJ opinion as non-complying.

**[02:47:43.300] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003953)  
Anyway, using the 25% formula,

**[02:47:46.940] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003954)  
ang nakolekta po dyan ng DSUD,

**[02:47:49.060] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003955)  
actually, ang nasa escrow accounts po dyan

**[02:47:52.120] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003956)  
ng mga developers,

**[02:47:54.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003957)  
around 1,300 escrow accounts po ito.

**[02:47:57.100] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003958)  
Ibig sabihin,

**[02:47:57.740] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003959)  
around 1,300 na projects

**[02:47:59.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003960)  
ang nag-avail nitong option na ito.

**[02:48:01.800] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003961)  
Nasa 15 billion po ang total.

**[02:48:05.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003962)  
Collection.

**[02:48:06.520] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003963)  
Ang collection.

**[02:48:07.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003964)  
15 billion.

**[02:48:10.340] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003965)  
So, meron po tayong mga projects ngayon

**[02:48:13.140] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003966)

**[02:48:17.720] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003967)

**[02:48:19.340] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003968)

**[02:48:23.100] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003969)

**[02:48:24.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003970)

**[02:48:26.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003971)

**[02:48:29.820] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003972)

**[02:48:31.960] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003973)

**[02:48:34.100] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003974)

**[02:48:37.420] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003975)

**[02:48:38.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003976)

**[02:48:41.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003977)

**[02:48:48.200] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003980)

**[02:48:54.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003982)

**[02:48:57.580] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003983)

**[02:49:15.520] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003992)

**[02:49:17.060] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003993)

**[02:49:20.620] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003994)

**[02:49:24.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003996)

**[02:49:26.460] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003997)

**[02:49:31.060] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003998)

**[02:49:33.180] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003999)

**[02:49:36.640] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004000)

**[02:49:39.520] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004001)

**[02:49:43.700] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004002)  
And then, yung iba po,

**[02:49:44.680] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004003)

**[02:49:46.880] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004004)

**[02:49:50.140] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004006)

**[02:49:52.180] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004007)  
Yung modular housing unit.

**[02:49:54.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004008)  
And then, we also use this

**[02:49:57.280] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004009)

**[02:49:59.040] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004010)

**[02:50:00.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004011)

**[02:50:02.760] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004012)

**[02:50:04.200] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004013)  
So, basically, yung po yung

**[02:50:05.480] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004014)

**[02:50:09.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004015)  
And that's good to know

**[02:50:11.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004016)

**[02:50:12.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004017)

**[02:50:14.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004018)

**[02:50:15.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004019)

**[02:50:18.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004020)

**[02:50:19.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004021)  
And I'm one of the authors of first

**[02:50:21.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004022)

**[02:50:24.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004023)

**[02:50:26.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004024)

**[02:50:28.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004025)

**[02:50:30.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004026)

**[02:50:31.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004027)  
Yun pa man, binabaan ng DSUD

**[02:50:33.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004028)  
ang prosyento ng

**[02:50:34.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004029)  
incentivized compliance

**[02:50:36.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004030)  
sa ilalim ng Department Order 2021

**[02:50:38.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004031)  
DAS 004

**[02:50:40.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004032)  
ginawa lang na lang

**[02:50:41.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004033)  
25% or 15% for subdivision

**[02:50:44.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004034)  
at 5% for condominium projects

**[02:50:47.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004035)  
ang pinapatupad na requirement.

**[02:50:50.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004036)  
So, and Sec, you mentioned

**[02:50:52.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004037)  
this already a while ago

**[02:50:53.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004038)  
that there are

**[02:50:54.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004039)  
two DOJ opinions issued

**[02:50:55.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004040)  
in 2024-2026

**[02:50:58.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004041)  
questioning the legality

**[02:51:00.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004042)  
of reducing

**[02:51:01.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004043)  
the incentivized compliance requirement

**[02:51:03.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004044)  
and allocating 15%

**[02:51:05.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004045)  
for admin expenses.

**[02:51:07.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004046)  
So, what action has DSUD taken

**[02:51:09.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004047)  
in response to these DOJ findings?

**[02:51:13.780] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004048)  
The latest, Mr. Chairman,

**[02:51:15.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004049)  
the latest DOJ opinion was

**[02:51:17.340] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004050)  
we received it in February 2026.

**[02:51:22.620] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004051)  
So, once after we received it,

**[02:51:24.020] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004052)  
we applied it prospectively.

**[02:51:25.960] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004053)  
So, sinunod namin yung DOJ opinion.

**[02:51:28.620] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004054)  
So, all future applications

**[02:51:32.740] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004055)  
na mag-a-avail nitong escrow option,

**[02:51:35.160] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004056)  
we already allowed,

**[02:51:37.520] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004057)  
we already removed the 25% factor.

**[02:51:42.660] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004058)  
Then, ito rin po,

**[02:51:45.660] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004059)  
ay na-issue ang kami ng COA

**[02:51:47.760] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004060)  
sometime in April or May 2026

**[02:51:50.400] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004061)  
saying na dapat i-apply namin yun retroactive.

**[02:51:54.020] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004062)  
So, knowing the impact

**[02:51:56.380] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004063)  
of that application,

**[02:51:58.200] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004064)  
the retroactive application

**[02:51:59.360] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004065)  
sa mga cash flow

**[02:52:00.360] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004066)  
ng mga developers,

**[02:52:02.040] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004067)  
we requested for a moratorium

**[02:52:04.180] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004068)  
sa Office of the President

**[02:52:05.360] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004069)  
which we received,

**[02:52:07.280] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004070)  
I think, last month,

**[02:52:08.720] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004071)  
a little over last month,

**[02:52:10.400] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004072)  
na inalaw yung

**[02:52:11.540] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004073)  
suspension

**[02:52:14.340] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004074)  
ng retroactive application.

**[02:52:17.560] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004075)  
Ibig sabihin nito,

**[02:52:18.300] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004076)  
yung assess and collection

**[02:52:19.480] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004077)  
nung mga dapat collect tayo

**[02:52:20.940] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004078)  
sa mga developers

**[02:52:21.940] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004079)  
hanggang February 2026.

**[02:52:24.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004080)  
2027.

**[02:52:27.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004081)  
So, moratorium until 2027?

**[02:52:29.560] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004082)  
Yes.

**[02:52:30.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004083)  
One year.

**[02:52:31.080] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004084)  
Ang sabi po dun sa moratorium

**[02:52:32.640] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004085)  
na approval ng Office of the President,

**[02:52:34.540] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004086)  
one year from the issuance

**[02:52:36.320] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004087)  
of the DOJ opinion

**[02:52:37.400] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004088)  
which was issued

**[02:52:38.540] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004089)  
February 2026.

**[02:52:40.440] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004090)  
So, February 2027 po,

**[02:52:42.780] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004091)  
mag-e-expire yung moratorium.

**[02:52:44.640] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004092)  
And at the same time,

**[02:52:46.100] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004093)  
we received also communications

**[02:52:48.040] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004094)  
from the urban poor community.

**[02:52:51.720] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004095)  
Of course,

**[02:52:52.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004096)  
alam naman natin

**[02:52:53.480] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004097)  
Ang output nitong escrow fund at the end of the day are all related to socialized housing. So ang magiging beneficiary nito, nakakaraming beneficiary nito ay yung nasa urban housing sector. So we are communicating with both the developers and the urban poor sector.

**[02:53:13.980] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004098)  
In fact, last week, we had a meeting with the private sector advisory council for real estate with the president. So we discussed on how to deal with this DOJ and COA issue, including the moratorium, Mr. Chair.

**[02:53:30.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004099)  
Okay, thank you. Ito pa, just two more points. Ito sa ano pa rin ito?

**[02:53:38.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004100)  
Developers express their concern that projects dating from as early as 2017 will be collected escrow.

**[02:53:43.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004101)  
Deficiency, even if they complied in good faith with the prevailing rules at the time.

**[02:53:48.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004102)  
These developers followed rules issued by the government and obtained approvals from the government.

**[02:53:53.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004103)  
If the issue itself issued a legally defective rule, is it fair to make the private sector shoulder the entire consequence years later?

**[02:54:02.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004104)  
So how does the issue intend to address this concern and ensure the fair implementation of the revised requirements?

**[02:54:09.580] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004105)  
Again, as I mentioned a while ago,

**[02:54:13.260] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004106)  
we

**[02:54:13.660] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004107)  
knowing the effect of this

**[02:54:16.060] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004108)  
retroactive application to the cash flow of developers, we requested for a moratorium, which was approved by the office of the president.

**[02:54:24.400] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004109)  
And then at the same time, we are also discussing with the urban poor on how to have a good way of proceeding with this matter, Mr. Chair.

**[02:54:34.980] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004110)  
And in fact, the office of the president is already on top of this matter.

**[02:54:41.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004111)  
Okay, thank you.

**[02:54:42.740] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004112)  
Thank you.

**[02:54:43.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004113)  
Ito naman sa housing programs for last point, housing programs for middle-income Filipinos under Republic Act No. 11201,

**[02:54:52.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004114)  
the issue is mandated to develop a national strategy to provide adequate affordable housing to all Filipinos.

**[02:54:58.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004115)  
While we recognize the importance of socialized housing for underprivileged families,

**[02:55:03.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004116)  
I would like to emphasize that our housing policy should not overlook the middle-income Filipino families.

**[02:55:08.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004117)  
Maraming din tayo mga middle-income earners, mga guro, government employees,

**[02:55:11.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004118)  
mga ordinary magagawa na may regular na trabaho at kita,

**[02:55:16.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004119)  
pero hirap na parin makabili na yung sariling bahay.

**[02:55:19.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004120)  
Hindi sila kawilipikado sa socialized housing, pero hindi rin nila kaya bumili na bahay sa kasilukoy ang presyo.

**[02:55:26.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004121)  
Ano po ang kongkretong programa ng DHSUD para sa ating mga middle-income earners?

**[02:55:31.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004122)  
Sa inyong proposed 2027 budget, may nakalaang pondo o partikular na programa upang matugunan ang housing needs

**[02:55:38.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004123)  
ng ating mga

**[02:55:41.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004129)  
middle-income

**[02:55:41.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004130)  
Filipino families.

**[02:55:43.040] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004131)  
Mr. Chair, yung hindi pa kaya

**[02:55:45.280] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004132)  
bubili ng socialized housing which is the

**[02:55:47.440] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004133)  
lowest

**[02:55:47.760] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004134)  
segment, then papasok yung

**[02:55:51.360] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004135)  
CMP and rental housing.

**[02:55:53.240] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004136)  
Yung mga middle class earners naman na

**[02:55:55.240] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004137)  
naghahangad na mas mataas

**[02:55:56.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004138)  
sa socialized housing,

**[02:55:59.640] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004139)  
meron po tayong dalawang programa dyan

**[02:56:01.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004140)  
sa pag-ibig fund. Kung bibili po

**[02:56:03.420] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004141)  
kayo ng unit na

**[02:56:05.100] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004142)  
hanggang 4.9 million pesos,

**[02:56:06.800] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004143)  
ang interest rate lang po natin is

**[02:56:08.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004144)  
4.5%. And then

**[02:56:10.820] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004145)  
up to 10 million pesos po, ang interest

**[02:56:12.980] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004146)  
natin is 5.75%.

**[02:56:14.900] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004147)  
This is way lower than the

**[02:56:16.960] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004148)  
present bank rates.

**[02:56:19.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004149)  
So, these are

**[02:56:20.660] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004150)  
subsidized rates.

**[02:56:22.640] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004151)

**[02:56:24.580] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-004152)
